  var g=el('g',{class:'bingoT parlorT',tabindex:0,role:'button','aria-label':'Bingo Hall',style:'cursor:pointer'},layers.tables);
  el('rect',{class:'pglow',x:L.x-w/2-8,y:L.y-h-8,width:w+16,height:h+10,rx:14,fill:'#fbbf24',opacity:0},g);
  el('image',{href:'https://saddleup-arcade.vercel.app/art/bingo_door.webp',x:L.x-w/2,y:L.y-h,width:w,height:h},g);
  el('rect',{x:L.x-w/2,y:L.y-h,width:w,height:h,fill:'transparent',style:'pointer-events:all'},g);
  function go(){try{parent.postMessage({arcade:'nav',to:'bingo'},'*');}catch(e){}}
  g.addEventListener('click',function(e){e.stopPropagation();go();});g.addEventListener('keydown',function(e){if(e.key==='Enter'||e.key===' '){e.preventDefault();go();}});
  g.addEventListener('mouseenter',function(){g.classList.add('hov');});g.addEventListener('mouseleave',function(){g.classList.remove('hov');});}
function parlorPt(){return {x:PARL.x+(rnd(30)-15),y:H-8,door:true};}
function botToParlor(b){var from=b.seat?seatPt(b.seat):{x:b.x,y:b.y},fcy=b.seat?centerY(b.seat):b.y-1;stand(b);walk(b,route(from,fcy,parlorPt(),H),85+rnd(25),function(){retire(b);});
  feed('<b>'+esc(b.name)+'</b> wandered off to the Game Parlor');}
function goParlor(){if(!PARL||going)return;going=true;closeBar();openRegs(false);select(null);hidePatron();if(MODE!=='desk')openLobby(false);if(!me)spawnMe(null);
  var tw=W/(LNAME==='tall'?1.6:2.6),to={x:PARL.x-tw/2,y:H-tw*H/W,w:tw,h:tw*H/W};feed('<b>You</b> are heading to the Game Parlor','live');
  var opened=false,open=function(){if(opened)return;opened=true;setTimeout(function(){going=false;try{parent.postMessage({arcade:'open',g:'parlor'},'*');}catch(e){}},motionOff()?0:350);};
  if(motionOff()){flyTo(to,0);open();return;}flyTo(to,1500);setTimeout(open,2000);
  var from=me.seat?seatPt(me.seat):{x:me.x,y:me.y,door:me.atDoor},fcy=me.seat?centerY(me.seat):(me.atDoor?H:me.y-1);stand(me);me.atDoor=false;
  var pts=route(from,fcy,parlorPt(),H),len=0,px=from.x,py=from.y;pts.forEach(function(q){len+=Math.hypot(q.x-px,q.y-py);px=q.x;py=q.y;});
  walk(me,pts,Math.max(300,len/2.1),function(){me.atDoor=true;open();});}

/* ---------- Lucky Spur Wheel podium (pure SVG) ---------- */
var WHEEL=null;
function drawWheel(){var L=LAY.wheel;if(!L){WHEEL=null;return;}var R=L.r,g=el('g',{class:'wheelT',transform:'translate('+L.x+' '+L.y+')',tabindex:0,role:'button','aria-label':'Lucky Spur Wheel, free spin daily'},layers.tables);
  WHEEL={x:L.x,y:L.y,r:R,g:g,spots:L.spots.map(function(a,i){return {x:a[0],y:a[1],a:a[2],occ:null,i:i,wheel:true};})};
  el('ellipse',{cx:0,cy:R*1.02,rx:R*1.12,ry:R*.34,fill:'rgba(0,0,0,.45)'},g);
  el('circle',{class:'wglow',r:R*1.32,fill:'#f2d27e',opacity:0},g);
  el('rect',{x:-R*.95,y:R*.72,width:R*1.9,height:R*.34,rx:R*.12,fill:'#5b3a1e',stroke:'#c6a75e','stroke-width':1.6},g);
  el('rect',{x:-R*.16,y:R*.2,width:R*.32,height:R*.6,fill:'#3b2414',stroke:'#c6a75e','stroke-width':1.2},g);
  el('circle',{r:R*1.1,fill:'#2a160c',stroke:'#c6a75e','stroke-width':3},g);
  el('circle',{r:R*1.1-4,fill:'none',stroke:'#f2d27e','stroke-width':1,opacity:.6},g);
  var nb=18;for(var i=0;i<nb;i++){var a=deg(i*360/nb-90);el('circle',{class:'lamp',cx:Math.cos(a)*R*1.03,cy:Math.sin(a)*R*1.03,r:Math.max(2.4,R*.05),fill:i%2?'#fde68a':'#fb923c',style:'animation-delay:'+(i%2?.55:0)+'s'},g);}
  var sw=el('g',{class:'wspin'},g),face=el('g',{class:'wslow'},sw);WHEEL.sw=sw;
  var cols=['#b91c1c','#1e3a8a','#047857','#ca8a04','#7c2d12','#1e3a8a','#047857','#b91c1c','#ca8a04','#1e3a8a','#7c3aed','#047857'],n=cols.length,rr=R*.92;
  for(var k=0;k<n;k++){var a0=deg(k*360/n-90),a1=deg((k+1)*360/n-90);
    el('path',{d:'M0 0L'+(Math.cos(a0)*rr).toFixed(2)+' '+(Math.sin(a0)*rr).toFixed(2)+'A'+rr+' '+rr+' 0 0 1 '+(Math.cos(a1)*rr).toFixed(2)+' '+(Math.sin(a1)*rr).toFixed(2)+'Z',fill:cols[k],stroke:'#f2d27e','stroke-width':1.1},face);
    var am=deg((k+.5)*360/n-90);el('circle',{cx:Math.cos(am)*rr*.72,cy:Math.sin(am)*rr*.72,r:R*.07,fill:'#fde68a',opacity:.9},face);}
  el('circle',{r:R*.3,fill:'#1c1208',stroke:'#f2d27e','stroke-width':2},face);
  el('path',{d:starPath(R*.2,R*.08),fill:'#f2d27e'},face);
  el('path',{d:'M0 '+(-R*.8)+'L'+(-R*.13)+' '+(-R*1.2)+'L'+(R*.13)+' '+(-R*1.2)+'Z',fill:'#fde68a',stroke:'#7c2d12','stroke-width':1.4},g);
  el('circle',{class:'hit wheelhit',r:R*1.2,fill:'transparent',style:'cursor:pointer;pointer-events:all'},g);
  var sg=el('g',{transform:'translate('+L.sign[0]+' '+L.sign[1]+') scale('+LAY.ss+')'},layers.signs),sw2=L.side?150:176,sh2=L.side?44:40;
  el('rect',{x:-sw2/2,y:-sh2/2,width:sw2,height:sh2,rx:9,fill:'rgba(10,8,6,.9)',stroke:'#f2d27e','stroke-width':1.6},sg);
  el('text',{x:0,y:L.side?-3:-2,'text-anchor':'middle','font-size':L.side?16:17,fill:'#f2d27e',class:'rye','letter-spacing':'1.5'},sg).textContent='LUCKY WHEEL';
  el('text',{x:0,y:L.side?13:14,'text-anchor':'middle','font-size':10,'font-weight':800,fill:'#86efac','letter-spacing':'1'},sg).textContent='FREE SPIN DAILY';
  g.addEventListener('mouseenter',function(){g.classList.add('hov');});g.addEventListener('mouseleave',function(){g.classList.remove('hov');});
  g.addEventListener('keydown',function(e){if(e.key==='Enter'||e.key===' '){e.preventDefault();goWheel();}});}
function starPath(ro,ri){var d='';for(var i=0;i<10;i++){var a=deg(i*36-90),r=i%2?ri:ro;d+=(i?'L':'M')+(Math.cos(a)*r).toFixed(2)+' '+(Math.sin(a)*r).toFixed(2);}return d+'Z';}
function spinWheel(){if(!WHEEL||motionOff())return;var turns=720+rnd(720);anim(WHEEL.sw,[{transform:'rotate(0deg)'},{transform:'rotate('+turns+'deg)'}],3200,{easing:'cubic-bezier(.15,.7,.2,1)',fill:'none'});}
function wheelTick(){if(!WHEEL||!running)return;
  WHEEL.spots.forEach(function(s){var p=s.occ&&people[s.occ];if(p&&p.kind==='bot'&&!p.path&&p.wheelAt&&Date.now()-p.wheelAt>5200){p.wheelAt=0;var ch=choices(null);if(ch.length&&chance(.75))goSeat(p,pick(ch));else goDoor(p,function(){retire(p);});}});
  var free=WHEEL.spots.filter(function(s){return !s.occ;});if(!free.length||!chance(.4))return;
  var cand=[];for(var id in people){var p=people[id];if(p.kind==='bot'&&!p.staff&&!p.path&&p.seat&&!p.seat.wheel)cand.push(p);}if(!cand.length)return;
  var b=pick(cand),s=pick(free);goSeat(b,s,function(){b.wheelAt=Date.now();setTimeout(function(){if(!running||b.seat!==s)return;spinWheel();
    setTimeout(function(){if(!running)return;var amt=pick([25,50,100,250,500]);feed('<b>'+esc(b.name)+'</b> spun the Lucky Wheel and won '+money(amt),'win');floatText(WHEEL.x,WHEEL.y-WHEEL.r-10,'+'+money(amt));},3300);},700);});
  feed('<b>'+esc(b.name)+'</b> is walking up to the Lucky Wheel');}
function goWheel(){if(!WHEEL||going)return;going=true;closeBar();openRegs(false);select(null);hidePatron();if(!me)spawnMe(null);
  var s=WHEEL.spots.filter(function(x){return !x.occ;})[0]||WHEEL.spots[0];if(s.occ&&people[s.occ]&&people[s.occ]!==me){var ob=people[s.occ];var ch=choices(null);if(ch.length)goSeat(ob,pick(ch));else goDoor(ob,function(){retire(ob);});}
  var tw=WHEEL.r*7,to={x:WHEEL.x-tw/2,y:WHEEL.y-tw*H/W/2,w:tw,h:tw*H/W};feed('<b>You</b> are heading to the Lucky Wheel','live');
  var opened=false,open=function(){if(opened)return;opened=true;spinWheel();setTimeout(function(){going=false;try{parent.postMessage({arcade:'open',g:'wheel'},'*');}catch(e){}},motionOff()?0:450);};
  if(motionOff()){flyTo(to,0);open();return;}flyTo(to,1600);setTimeout(open,2400);if(me.seat)stand(me);s.occ=null;goSeat(me,s,open);}

/* ---------- table life: dealing, dice, wins ---------- */
function anim(e,frames,ms,opts){try{return e.animate(frames,Object.assign({duration:ms,easing:'cubic-bezier(.2,.8,.3,1)',fill:'forwards'},opts||{}));}catch(_){return null;}}
function seated(T){return T.seats.filter(function(s){return s.occ&&people[s.occ]&&!people[s.occ].path;});}
function deal(T){if(motionOff())return;var occ=seated(T);if(!occ.length||!T.geo.shoe)return;var sh=T.geo.shoe,made=[],k=0;
  var targets=[];occ.forEach(function(s){targets.push(T.spots[s.i]);targets.push(T.spots[s.i]);});
  if(T.kind.type==='holdem'){var cm=T.geo.comm||{x:-46,dx:23,y:-5};for(var c=0;c<5;c++)targets.push({x:cm.x+c*cm.dx,y:cm.y,comm:true});}
  if(T.kind.type==='bj'||T.kind.type==='bacc'||T.kind.type==='war'){var dy=T.geo.dcard!=null?T.geo.dcard:T.geo.dealer.y+40;targets.push({x:-8,y:dy});targets.push({x:8,y:dy});}
  targets.forEach(function(t,i){setTimeout(function(){if(!running)return;var c=card(T.items,0,0,rnd(30)-15);made.push(c);var off=t.comm?0:(i%2?7:-1);
    anim(c,[{transform:'translate('+sh.x+'px,'+sh.y+'px) scale(.6)'},{transform:'translate('+(t.x+off)+'px,'+(t.y+(t.comm?0:-2))+'px) scale(1)'}],380);},i*140+k);});
  setTimeout(function(){made.forEach(function(c){var a=anim(c,[{opacity:1},{opacity:0}],400);setTimeout(function(){c.remove();},420);});},targets.length*140+3600);}
function dice(T){if(motionOff()||!T.dice)return;var sd=chance(.5)?1:-1,y0=(rnd(60)-30);
  for(var i=0;i<2;i++){(function(i){var d=el('g',null,T.dice);el('image',{href:DICE_ART[1+rnd(6)],x:-6,y:-6,width:12,height:12},d);
    anim(d,[{transform:'translate(0px,70px) rotate(0deg)'},{transform:'translate('+(sd*120)+'px,'+(y0+i*14)+'px) rotate(400deg)',offset:.6},{transform:'translate('+(sd*178)+'px,'+(y0+i*14-10)+'px) rotate(560deg)',offset:.8},{transform:'translate('+(sd*160)+'px,'+(y0+i*16)+'px) rotate(620deg)'}],1300);
    setTimeout(function(){d.remove();},3400);})(i);}}
function floatText(x,y,txt,col){var t=el('text',{x:x,y:y,'text-anchor':'middle','font-size':16,'font-weight':900,fill:col||'#fde68a',stroke:'#000','stroke-width':.6,'paint-order':'stroke'},layers.fx);t.textContent=txt;
  if(motionOff()){setTimeout(function(){t.remove();},1600);return;}
  anim(t,[{transform:'translateY(0)',opacity:0},{transform:'translateY(-10px)',opacity:1,offset:.15},{transform:'translateY(-46px)',opacity:0}],2200,{easing:'ease-out'});setTimeout(function(){t.remove();},2300);
  var b=el('g',{transform:'translate('+x+' '+(y+18)+')'},layers.fx);for(var i=0;i<10;i++){var a=deg(i*36),s=el('circle',{r:2.4,fill:i%2?'#fde68a':'#f59e0b'},b);
    anim(s,[{transform:'translate(0,0)',opacity:1},{transform:'translate('+(Math.cos(a)*36)+'px,'+(Math.sin(a)*36)+'px)',opacity:0}],900,{easing:'ease-out'});}
  setTimeout(function(){b.remove();},950);}
var lastWin=0;
function bigWin(){var tids=Object.keys(TB).filter(function(k){var y=TB[k].kind.type;return y!=='pinball'&&y!=='crash'&&y!=='derby'&&y!=='pool'&&y!=='backgammon';}),T=TB[pick(tids)],occ=seated(T).filter(function(s){return people[s.occ].kind==='bot';});if(!occ.length)return;
  var s=pick(occ),p=people[s.occ],mult=T.kind.type==='slots'?pick([20,40,75,150]):T.kind.type==='craps'?pick([4,6,10]):pick([2,3,5]),amt=pick([5,10,25,50])*mult;
  if(T.kind.type==='slots'&&chance(.08)){amt=2500+rnd(8000);slotJackpot(T);feed('<b>'+esc(p.name)+'</b> hit a <b>JACKPOT</b> on Stampede! '+money(amt),'win');}
  else feed('<b>'+esc(p.name)+'</b> won '+money(amt)+' at '+T.kind.name,'win');
  floatText(p.x,p.y-20,'+'+money(amt));}
function slotJackpot(T){if(motionOff())return;var r=el('rect',{x:T.x-190,y:T.y-120,width:380,height:240,rx:30,fill:'none',stroke:'#fde68a','stroke-width':6},layers.fx);
  anim(r,[{opacity:0},{opacity:1},{opacity:0},{opacity:1},{opacity:0}],2000);setTimeout(function(){r.remove();},2050);}

/* ---------- pool tables (pure SVG) + their table life ---------- */
var PBALL=['#facc15','#1d4ed8','#dc2626','#7c3aed','#f97316','#15803d','#881337','#111827'],PFX=44,PFY=94,BR=3.3;
var POCK=[[-45.5,-95.5],[45.5,-95.5],[-48,0],[48,0],[-45.5,95.5],[45.5,95.5]];
function drawPool(T,f){var m=T.geo.seats[1].x>0?1:-1;
  el('rect',{x:-58,y:-106,width:124,height:224,rx:14,fill:'rgba(0,0,0,.42)'},f);
  el('rect',{x:-58,y:-108,width:116,height:216,rx:12,fill:'url(#poolR)',stroke:'#140a04','stroke-width':2},f);
  el('rect',{x:-53.5,y:-103.5,width:107,height:207,rx:9,fill:'none',stroke:'#c6a75e','stroke-width':.9,opacity:.55},f);
  el('rect',{x:-48.5,y:-98.5,width:97,height:197,rx:3,fill:'#0a5232'},f);
  el('rect',{x:-PFX,y:-PFY,width:PFX*2,height:PFY*2,fill:'url(#poolF)'},f);
  [23.5,47,70.5].forEach(function(y){[-1,1].forEach(function(sx){[-1,1].forEach(function(sy){el('path',{d:'M0 -2.6L1.7 0L0 2.6L-1.7 0Z',fill:'#f2d27e',transform:'translate('+(sx*53.2)+' '+(sy*y)+')'},f);});});});
  [-22,0,22].forEach(function(x){[-1,1].forEach(function(sy){el('path',{d:'M-2.6 0L0 1.7L2.6 0L0 -1.7Z',fill:'#f2d27e',transform:'translate('+x+' '+(sy*103.2)+')'},f);});});
  POCK.forEach(function(p,i){el('circle',{cx:p[0],cy:p[1],r:i===2||i===3?6.4:7,fill:'#050302',stroke:'#c6a75e','stroke-width':.8},f);});
  el('line',{x1:-PFX,x2:PFX,y1:47,y2:47,stroke:'#fff',opacity:.12,'stroke-width':.8},f);el('circle',{cx:0,cy:-47,r:1,fill:'#fff',opacity:.35},f);
  /* a spare cue resting on the far rail */
  var sc=el('g',{transform:'translate('+(-m*55)+' 14) rotate('+(m*3)+')'},f);el('rect',{x:-1,y:-80,width:2,height:58,rx:1,fill:'#e8cf9a'},sc);el('rect',{x:-1.4,y:-22,width:2.8,height:30,rx:1.2,fill:'#3b1d12'},sc);el('rect',{x:-1,y:-81.5,width:2,height:2.2,fill:'#60a5fa'},sc);
  var P={ballG:el('g',null,f),balls:[],busy:false,racked:false,nine:false};T.pool=P;
  var st=el('g',{opacity:0},f),sti=el('g',null,st);P.stick=st;P.sti=sti;
  el('rect',{x:-1,y:4,width:2,height:52,rx:1,fill:'#ecd7a6',stroke:'rgba(0,0,0,.35)','stroke-width':.3},sti);el('rect',{x:-1.5,y:56,width:3,height:30,rx:1.4,fill:'#3b1d12'},sti);
  el('rect',{x:-1.6,y:66,width:3.2,height:3,fill:'#c6a75e'},sti);el('rect',{x:-1,y:3,width:2,height:2.2,fill:'#60a5fa'},sti);
  /* hanging lamp: brass rod, two green shades and a warm glow */
  var lg=el('g',{transform:'translate('+T.x+' '+T.y+') scale('+T.s+')'},layers.light);
  el('ellipse',{cx:0,cy:0,rx:96,ry:150,fill:'url(#lampG)',style:'mix-blend-mode:screen'},lg);
  el('line',{x1:0,x2:0,y1:-84,y2:84,stroke:'#8a6a2c','stroke-width':1.6,opacity:.55},lg);
  [-74,74].forEach(function(y){el('ellipse',{cx:0,cy:y,rx:12,ry:9,fill:'#14532d',stroke:'#c6a75e','stroke-width':1,opacity:.92},lg);el('circle',{cx:0,cy:y,r:3.2,fill:'#fff7d6',opacity:.9},lg);});
  poolRack(T,chance(.35));}
function ballEl(P,n){var g=el('g',null,P.ballG);
  if(n===0)el('circle',{r:BR,fill:'#fbfaf3',stroke:'#cbd5e1','stroke-width':.4},g);
  else if(n>8){el('circle',{r:BR,fill:'#fbfaf3',stroke:'rgba(0,0,0,.35)','stroke-width':.4},g);el('ellipse',{rx:BR*.98,ry:1.7,fill:PBALL[n-9]},g);}
  else{el('circle',{r:BR,fill:PBALL[n-1],stroke:'rgba(0,0,0,.45)','stroke-width':.4},g);if(n===8)el('circle',{r:1.2,fill:'#fff'},g);}
  el('circle',{cx:-1,cy:-1.1,r:.9,fill:'#fff',opacity:.65},g);return g;}
function bpos(b,x,y,k){b.x=x;b.y=y;b.g.style.transform='translate('+x.toFixed(2)+'px,'+y.toFixed(2)+'px)'+(k!=null?' scale('+k+')':'');}
function bslide(b,x,y,ms,ez,k){var from=b.g.style.transform||'translate(0px,0px)';var a=anim(b.g,[{transform:from},{transform:'translate('+x.toFixed(2)+'px,'+y.toFixed(2)+'px)'+(k!=null?' scale('+k+')':'')}],ms,{easing:ez||'cubic-bezier(.15,.7,.35,1)',fill:'none'});bpos(b,x,y,k);return a;}
function freeSpot(P,minD){for(var t=0;t<60;t++){var x=-38+rnd(77),y=-86+rnd(173),ok=true;P.balls.forEach(function(b){if(b.alive&&Math.hypot(b.x-x,b.y-y)<minD)ok=false;});POCK.forEach(function(p){if(Math.hypot(p[0]-x,p[1]-y)<14)ok=false;});if(ok)return {x:x,y:y};}return {x:-30+rnd(60),y:-60+rnd(120)};}
/* full=true: fresh rack waiting for the break. Otherwise a game in progress. Alternates 8-ball and 9-ball. */
function poolRack(T,full){var P=T.pool;while(P.ballG.firstChild)P.ballG.removeChild(P.ballG.firstChild);P.balls=[];P.nine=chance(.3);
  var cue={n:0,alive:true,g:ballEl(P,0)};P.balls.push(cue);P.cue=cue;
  var nums=[];if(P.nine){nums=[1,2,3,4,5,6,7,8,9];}else for(var i=1;i<=15;i++)nums.push(i);
  if(full){P.racked=true;bpos(cue,0,60+rnd(10));var d=2*BR+.25,rows=P.nine?[1,2,3,2,1]:[1,2,3,4,5],order;
    if(P.nine){order=[1].concat(nums.slice(1,8).sort(function(){return rnd(3)-1;}));order.splice(4,0,9);}else{order=nums.filter(function(n){return n!==8;}).sort(function(){return rnd(3)-1;});order.splice(4,0,8);}
    var idx=0;rows.forEach(function(c,r){for(var k=0;k<c;k++){var n=order[idx++];if(n==null)continue;var b={n:n,alive:true,g:ballEl(P,n)};bpos(b,(k-(c-1)/2)*d,-40-r*d*.87);P.balls.push(b);}});}
  else{P.racked=false;var keep=P.nine?2+rnd(4):4+rnd(5),pool2=nums.filter(function(n){return n!==(P.nine?9:8);}).sort(function(){return rnd(3)-1;}).slice(0,keep-1);pool2.push(P.nine?9:8);
    var c0=freeSpot(P,10);bpos(cue,c0.x,c0.y);pool2.forEach(function(n){var b={n:n,alive:true,g:ballEl(P,n)};P.balls.push(b);var q=freeSpot(P,10);bpos(b,q.x,q.y);});}}
function nearPocket(b){var best=null,bd=1e9;POCK.forEach(function(p){var d=Math.hypot(p[0]-b.x,p[1]-b.y)+rnd(30);if(d<bd){bd=d;best=p;}});return best;}
function clampF(x,y){return {x:Math.max(-PFX+BR+1,Math.min(PFX-BR-1,x)),y:Math.max(-PFY+BR+1,Math.min(PFY-BR-1,y))};}
function poolShot(T){var P=T.pool;if(!P||P.busy||motionOff()||!running)return;var occ=seated(T);if(!occ.length)return;
  var shooter=people[pick(occ).occ];if(!shooter)return;P.busy=true;
  var mine=function(){return running&&TB[T.id]===T;};
  shooter.g.classList.add('show');setTimeout(function(){if(!(hovT===T))shooter.g.classList.remove('show');},2600);
  var C=P.cue,objs=P.balls.filter(function(b){return b.alive&&b.n;}),last=P.nine?9:8,tgt,G,u;
  if(P.racked){tgt=null;G={x:0,y:-36};}
  else{var cand=P.nine?[objs.slice().sort(function(a,b){return a.n-b.n;})[0]]:objs.filter(function(b){return b.n!==last;});if(!cand.length||!cand[0])cand=objs;tgt=pick(cand);
    var pk=nearPocket(tgt),dx=pk[0]-tgt.x,dy=pk[1]-tgt.y,dl=Math.hypot(dx,dy)||1;tgt.pk=pk;tgt.d={x:dx/dl,y:dy/dl};G=clampF(tgt.x-tgt.d.x*2*BR,tgt.y-tgt.d.y*2*BR);}
  var ux=G.x-C.x,uy=G.y-C.y,ul=Math.hypot(ux,uy);if(ul<3){var q=freeSpot(P,12);bpos(C,q.x,q.y);P.busy=false;return;}ux/=ul;uy/=ul;
  /* line up: the cue stick slides in behind the cue ball, draws back twice and strikes */
  P.stick.setAttribute('transform','translate('+C.x.toFixed(2)+' '+C.y.toFixed(2)+') rotate('+(Math.atan2(ux,-uy)*180/Math.PI).toFixed(1)+')');
  anim(P.stick,[{opacity:0},{opacity:1}],250);
  anim(P.sti,[{transform:'translate(0px,26px)'},{transform:'translate(0px,10px)',offset:.35},{transform:'translate(0px,22px)',offset:.6},{transform:'translate(0px,12px)',offset:.8},{transform:'translate(0px,26px)',offset:.92},{transform:'translate(0px,0px)'}],1150,{easing:'ease-in-out'});
  setTimeout(function(){if(!mine()){P.busy=false;return;}
    setTimeout(function(){anim(P.stick,[{opacity:1},{opacity:0}],350);},250);
    var t1=Math.min(700,140+ul*5);bslide(C,G.x,G.y,t1,'cubic-bezier(.3,.6,.5,1)');
    setTimeout(function(){if(!mine()){P.busy=false;return;}
      if(P.racked){P.racked=false;var drop=[],obs=P.balls.filter(function(b){return b.n;});
        obs.forEach(function(b){b.alive=false;});
        var cands=obs.filter(function(b){return b.n!==last;}).sort(function(){return rnd(3)-1;});drop=cands.slice(0,1+rnd(2));
        obs.forEach(function(b){b.alive=true;var q=drop.indexOf(b)>=0?null:freeSpot(P,9);if(q)bslide(b,q.x,q.y,700+rnd(500));else{var pk=nearPocket(b);b.alive=false;bslide(b,pk[0],pk[1],600+rnd(300));sinkBall(P,b,900);}});
        var cq=freeSpot(P,10);bslide(C,cq.x,cq.y,900,'cubic-bezier(.2,.7,.4,1)');
        feed('<b>'+esc(shooter.name)+'</b> broke the rack at '+(P.nine?'9-ball':'8-ball'));
        setTimeout(function(){P.busy=false;},1200);return;}
      var miss=chance(.18),ex=tgt.pk[0],ey=tgt.pk[1],dd=Math.hypot(ex-tgt.x,ey-tgt.y);
      if(miss){var s2=clampF(tgt.x+tgt.d.x*dd*.65+(rnd(20)-10),tgt.y+tgt.d.y*dd*.65+(rnd(20)-10));bslide(tgt,s2.x,s2.y,300+dd*5);}
      else{tgt.alive=false;bslide(tgt,ex,ey,Math.min(800,180+dd*6),'cubic-bezier(.25,.6,.5,1)');sinkBall(P,tgt,Math.min(800,180+dd*6));}
      /* cue ball carries on along the tangent and stops */
      var dot=ux*tgt.d.x+uy*tgt.d.y,tx=ux-dot*tgt.d.x,ty=uy-dot*tgt.d.y,cs=clampF(C.x+tx*(18+rnd(30))+ux*4,C.y+ty*(18+rnd(30))+uy*4);bslide(C,cs.x,cs.y,600,'cubic-bezier(.2,.7,.4,1)');
      setTimeout(function(){if(!mine()){P.busy=false;return;}
        if(!miss&&tgt.n===last){var amt=pick([10,20,25,50,100]);feed('<b>'+esc(shooter.name)+'</b> sank the '+last+' \u2014 Pool win!','win');floatText(shooter.x,shooter.y-20,'+'+money(amt));
          setTimeout(function(){if(!mine())return;anim(P.ballG,[{opacity:1},{opacity:0}],400);setTimeout(function(){if(!mine())return;poolRack(T,true);anim(P.ballG,[{opacity:0},{opacity:1}],400);P.busy=false;},420);},1400);}
        else{if(!miss&&chance(.3))setTimeout(function(){P.busy=false;poolShot(T);},500);else P.busy=false;}},Math.min(900,260+dd*6));},t1);},1150);}
function sinkBall(P,b,after){setTimeout(function(){var a=anim(b.g,[{transform:b.g.style.transform,opacity:1},{transform:b.g.style.transform+' scale(.35)',opacity:0}],260,{easing:'ease-in'});setTimeout(function(){if(b.g.parentNode)b.g.remove();},270);},after);}

/* ---------- backgammon tables (pure SVG) + their table life ---------- */
var BGW=6.333;
function bgXY(n,k){var x,y;if(n<=12){x=n<=6?42-(n-.5)*BGW:-4-(n-6.5)*BGW;y=29.6-Math.min(k,4)*5.7+(k>4?2.8:0);}else{var m=n-12;x=m<=6?-42+(m-.5)*BGW:4+(m-6.5)*BGW;y=-29.6+Math.min(k,4)*5.7-(k>4?2.8:0);}return {x:x,y:y};}
function drawBG(T,f){var R=el('g',{transform:'rotate('+(T.geo.rot||0)+')'},f);
  el('rect',{x:-50,y:-42,width:104,height:90,rx:8,fill:'rgba(0,0,0,.42)'},R);
  el('rect',{x:-52,y:-45,width:104,height:90,rx:8,fill:'url(#bgWood)',stroke:'#24120a','stroke-width':2},R);
  el('rect',{x:-48.5,y:-41.5,width:97,height:83,rx:6,fill:'none',stroke:'#c6a75e','stroke-width':.8,opacity:.55},R);
  el('rect',{x:-45.5,y:-36,width:91,height:72,rx:3,fill:'#3b2212',stroke:'#c6a75e','stroke-width':.9},R);
  [-42,4].forEach(function(x0){el('rect',{x:x0,y:-33,width:38,height:66,fill:'#14532d'},R);
    for(var i=0;i<6;i++){var x=x0+i*BGW;el('path',{d:'M'+x.toFixed(2)+' -33L'+(x+BGW).toFixed(2)+' -33L'+(x+BGW/2).toFixed(2)+' -7Z',fill:i%2?'#9f1239':'#f2d27e',opacity:.92},R);
      el('path',{d:'M'+x.toFixed(2)+' 33L'+(x+BGW).toFixed(2)+' 33L'+(x+BGW/2).toFixed(2)+' 7Z',fill:i%2?'#f2d27e':'#9f1239',opacity:.92},R);}});
  el('rect',{x:-4,y:-36,width:8,height:72,fill:'#24120a'},R);[-22,22].forEach(function(y){el('rect',{x:-1.6,y:y-3,width:3.2,height:6,rx:.8,fill:'#c6a75e'},R);});
  var cube=el('g',{transform:'translate(0 0)'},R);el('rect',{x:-3.6,y:-3.6,width:7.2,height:7.2,rx:1.2,fill:'#fef3c7',stroke:'#1c1917','stroke-width':.4},cube);
  var ct=el('text',{x:0,y:1.9,'text-anchor':'middle','font-size':5,'font-weight':900,fill:'#1c1917'},cube);ct.textContent='64';
  T.bg={R:R,cg:el('g',null,R),dg:el('g',null,R),cube:ct,busy:false,turn:rnd(2),pts:[],off:[0,0],cv:1};
  bgReset(T,chance(.6));}
function chk(B,c){var g=el('g',null,B.cg);el('circle',{r:2.85,fill:c?'#1c1917':'#fdf6e3',stroke:c?'#c6a75e':'#a8a29e','stroke-width':.6},g);el('circle',{r:1.5,fill:'none',stroke:c?'#c6a75e':'#d6d3d1','stroke-width':.4,opacity:.8},g);return g;}
function bgReset(T,race){var B=T.bg;while(B.cg.firstChild)B.cg.removeChild(B.cg.firstChild);B.pts=[];for(var i=0;i<=25;i++)B.pts.push({c:-1,l:[]});B.off=[0,0];B.cv=1;B.cube.textContent='64';
  var lay=race?[[[6,3],[5,3],[4,2],[3,3],[2,2],[1,2]],[[19,3],[20,3],[21,3],[22,2],[23,2],[24,2]]]:[[[24,2],[13,5],[8,3],[6,5]],[[1,2],[12,5],[17,3],[19,5]]];
  if(race){lay[0][rnd(6)][1]+=0;var a=rnd(3);lay[0][a][1]--;lay[0].push([7+rnd(3),1]);var b=rnd(3);lay[1][b][1]--;lay[1].push([16+rnd(3),1]);}
  lay.forEach(function(L,c){L.forEach(function(q){for(var k=0;k<q[1];k++){var p=B.pts[q[0]];p.c=c;var g=chk(B,c),xy=bgXY(q[0],p.l.length);p.l.push(g);g.style.transform='translate('+xy.x.toFixed(2)+'px,'+xy.y.toFixed(2)+'px)';}});});}
function bgMove(B,c,die){var dir=c?1:-1,list=[],home=true,hi=c?25:0;
  for(var n=1;n<=24;n++){var p=B.pts[n];if(p.c===c&&p.l.length){if(c?n<19:n>6)home=false;if(c?n<hi:n>hi)hi=n;}}
  for(var n2=1;n2<=24;n2++){var q=B.pts[n2];if(q.c!==c||!q.l.length)continue;var d=n2+dir*die;
    if(d>=1&&d<=24){var t=B.pts[d];if(t.c===c||!t.l.length||t.l.length===1)list.push({f:n2,t:d,hit:t.c!==c&&t.l.length===1});}
    else if(home&&(d===0||d===25||n2===hi))list.push({f:n2,t:-1});}
  if(!list.length)return null;var hits=list.filter(function(m){return m.hit;}),offs=list.filter(function(m){return m.t<0;});
  return offs.length&&chance(.85)?pick(offs):hits.length&&chance(.6)?pick(hits):pick(list);}
function bgTurn(T){var B=T.bg;if(!B||B.busy||motionOff()||!running)return;var occ=seated(T);if(!occ.length)return;B.busy=true;
  var mine=function(){return running&&TB[T.id]===T;},c=B.turn,d1=1+rnd(6),d2=1+rnd(6),sd=c?-1:1;B.turn=1-c;
  [d1,d2].forEach(function(v,i){var d=el('g',null,B.dg);el('rect',{x:-3.4,y:-3.4,width:6.8,height:6.8,rx:1.3,fill:'#fff',stroke:'#94a3b8','stroke-width':.3},d);el('image',{href:DICE_ART[v],x:-3.4,y:-3.4,width:6.8,height:6.8},d);
    var lx=sd*(16+i*9),ly=sd*(12-i*5);anim(d,[{transform:'translate('+(sd*8)+'px,'+(sd*46)+'px) rotate(0deg)'},{transform:'translate('+(lx+sd*3)+'px,'+(ly-sd*4)+'px) rotate('+(300+rnd(200))+'deg)',offset:.7},{transform:'translate('+lx+'px,'+ly+'px) rotate('+(rnd(40)-20)+'deg)'}],700,{easing:'ease-out'});
    setTimeout(function(){anim(d,[{opacity:1},{opacity:0}],300);setTimeout(function(){d.remove();},320);},2300);});
  if(chance(.04)&&B.cv<16){B.cv*=2;B.cube.textContent=String(B.cv);}
  var dice=d1===d2?[d1,d1,d1,d1]:[d1,d2],mi=0;
  (function step(){if(!mine()){B.busy=false;return;}if(mi>=dice.length){B.busy=false;return;}var mv=bgMove(B,c,dice[mi++]);if(!mv){B.busy=false;return;}
    var fp=B.pts[mv.f],g=fp.l.pop();if(!fp.l.length)fp.c=-1;var from=g.style.transform;
    if(mv.t<0){B.off[c]++;anim(g,[{transform:from,opacity:1},{transform:'translate('+(c?-50:50)+'px,'+(c?-20:20)+'px)',opacity:0}],450,{easing:'ease-in'});setTimeout(function(){g.remove();},460);}
    else{var tp=B.pts[mv.t];if(mv.hit){var hg=tp.l.pop(),hc=tp.c,hf=hg.style.transform;tp.c=-1;anim(hg,[{transform:hf},{transform:'translate(0px,'+(hc?-9:9)+'px) scale(1.2)'}],380);
        setTimeout(function(){if(!mine())return;var en=hc?1+rnd(6):19+rnd(6),ep=B.pts[en];if(ep.c!==hc&&ep.l.length){en=hc?1:24;ep=B.pts[en];}if(ep.c!==hc&&ep.l.length){hg.remove();return;}ep.c=hc;var xy=bgXY(en,ep.l.length);ep.l.push(hg);
          anim(hg,[{transform:'translate(0px,'+(hc?-9:9)+'px) scale(1.2)'},{transform:'translate('+xy.x.toFixed(2)+'px,'+xy.y.toFixed(2)+'px)'}],500);hg.style.transform='translate('+xy.x.toFixed(2)+'px,'+xy.y.toFixed(2)+'px)';},1300);}
      tp.c=c;var xy=bgXY(mv.t,tp.l.length);tp.l.push(g);var to='translate('+xy.x.toFixed(2)+'px,'+xy.y.toFixed(2)+'px)';
      anim(g,[{transform:from},{transform:'translate('+((parseFloat(from.split('(')[1])+xy.x)/2).toFixed(2)+'px,'+((parseFloat(from.split(',')[1])+xy.y)/2).toFixed(2)+'px) scale(1.45)',offset:.5},{transform:to}],420,{easing:'ease-in-out'});g.style.transform=to;}
    if(B.off[c]>=15){var w=people[pick(occ).occ],gam=B.off[1-c]===0,amt=pick([10,25,50])*B.cv*(gam?2:1);if(w){feed('<b>'+esc(w.name)+'</b> '+(gam?'won a gammon':'won')+' at Backgammon','win');floatText(w.x,w.y-20,'+'+money(amt));}
      setTimeout(function(){if(!mine())return;anim(B.cg,[{opacity:1},{opacity:0}],400);setTimeout(function(){if(!mine())return;bgReset(T,chance(.7));anim(B.cg,[{opacity:0},{opacity:1}],400);B.busy=false;},420);},1600);return;}
    setTimeout(step,480);})();}

/* ---------- regulars move around ---------- */
function openSeats(T){return T.seats.filter(function(s){return !s.occ;});}
function totalSeats(){var n=0;for(var k in TB)n+=TB[k].seats.length;return n;}
function botsAt(T){return T.seats.filter(function(s){return s.occ&&people[s.occ]&&people[s.occ].kind==='bot'&&people[s.occ].seat===s;}).map(function(s){return people[s.occ];});}
function choices(exclude){var arr=[];for(var k in TB){var T=TB[k];if(T===exclude||T.humans.length>=2)continue;openSeats(T).forEach(function(s){arr.push(s);});}return arr;}
function lifeTick(){if(!running)return;
  var bots=[];for(var id in people){var p=people[id];if(p.kind==='bot'&&!p.path)bots.push(p);}
  var seatedBots=bots.filter(function(b){return b.seat&&!b.seat.bar&&!b.seat.wheel;}),target=Math.round(totalSeats()*.62);
  var moves=chance(.4)?2:1;for(var mv=0;mv<moves;mv++)if(chance(.8)&&seatedBots.length){var b=seatedBots.splice(rnd(seatedBots.length),1)[0],from=tableOfSeat(b.seat),r=rnd(100);
    if(r<66){var ch=choices(from);if(ch.length){var s=pick(ch),tn=tableOfSeat(s).kind.name;goSeat(b,s);feed('<b>'+esc(b.name)+'</b> '+(from&&from.kind.name!==tn?'left '+from.kind.name+' and is heading to '+tn:'is switching seats at '+tn));}}
    else if(r<82){var bs=BAR.seats.filter(function(s){return !s.occ;});if(bs.length){goSeat(b,pick(bs));feed('<b>'+esc(b.name)+'</b> stepped away for a drink');}}
    else if(PARL&&chance(.4))botToParlor(b);
    else{goDoor(b,function(){retire(b);});feed('<b>'+esc(b.name)+'</b> cashed out and headed home');}}
  var barBots=bots.filter(function(b){return b.seat&&b.seat.bar;});
  if(barBots.length&&chance(.35)){var bb=pick(barBots),c2=choices(null);if(c2.length){var s2=pick(c2);goSeat(bb,s2);feed('<b>'+esc(bb.name)+'</b> sat down at '+tableOfSeat(s2).kind.name);}}
  if(seatedBots.length<target&&chance(.6)){var nb=botFromPool();if(nb){var c3=choices(null);if(c3.length){var fromP=PARL&&chance(.3),d=fromP?{x:PARL.x}:LAY.door;place(nb,d.x+rnd(40)-20,H-4,0);nb.atDoor=true;var s3=pick(c3);goSeat(nb,s3);feed('<b>'+esc(nb.name)+'</b> '+(fromP?'came back from the Game Parlor and sat down at ':'walked in and grabbed a seat at ')+tableOfSeat(s3).kind.name);}else retire(nb);}}
  updateSigns();}
var timers=[];
function every(ms,fn,jit){var t={id:0};(function loop(){t.id=setTimeout(function(){if(running)fn();loop();},ms+rnd(jit||1));})();timers.push(t);}
function stopTimers(){timers.forEach(function(t){clearTimeout(t.id);});timers=[];}

/* ---------- live players from the real tables ---------- */
var LV=null,liveMap={},myId=null;
function onLive(map){liveMap=map;syncHumans();}
function syncHumans(){
  var want={};
  var usedCh={};
  for(var tid in TB){var T=TB[tid],ch=T.kind.live;if(!ch||usedCh[ch]){T.humans=[];continue;}usedCh[ch]=1;
    var list=(liveMap[ch]||[]).filter(function(h){return h.id!==myId&&!want['h:'+h.id];});T.humans=list.slice(0,T.seats.length);
    T.humans.forEach(function(h){want['h:'+h.id]={h:h,T:T};});}
  for(var id in people){var p=people[id];if(p.kind==='human'&&!want[id]){feed('<b>'+esc(p.name)+'</b> left the table','live');goDoor(p,(function(pp){return function(){removePerson(pp);};})(p));p.kind='leaving';}}
  Object.keys(want).forEach(function(id){var w=want[id],T=w.T,p=people[id];
    if(p&&p.kind==='human'){if(p.lblText&&p.lblText.textContent!==w.h.name)p.lblText.textContent=w.h.name;if(p.seat&&tableOfSeat(p.seat)===T)return;}
    if(!p){p=mkPerson({id:id,name:w.h.name,col:'hsl('+hue(w.h.id)+',70%,55%)',skin:pick(SKIN),hat:chance(.5)?pick(HATS):null,hair:pick(HAIR),kind:'human'});
      place(p,LAY.door.x,H-4,0);p.atDoor=true;feed('<b>'+esc(w.h.name)+'</b> is playing '+T.kind.name+' live','live');}
    var s=openSeats(T)[0];
    if(!s){var bs=botsAt(T);if(bs.length){var b=bs[0];s=b.seat;evict(b);}}
    if(s)goSeat(p,s);});
  for(var t2 in TB){var TT=TB[t2];if(TT.humans.length>=2){var bb=botsAt(TT);if(bb.length){bb.forEach(evict);feed('Regulars cleared out for the live game at <b>'+TT.kind.name+'</b>','live');}}}
  updateSigns();}
function evict(b){var ch=choices(b.seat?tableOfSeat(b.seat):null),bar=BAR.seats.filter(function(s){return !s.occ;});
  if(ch.length&&chance(.6))goSeat(b,pick(ch));else if(bar.length)goSeat(b,pick(bar));else goDoor(b,function(){retire(b);});}

/* ---------- HUD ---------- */
function esc(s){return String(s).replace(/[&<>"]/g,function(c){return {'&':'&amp;','<':'&lt;','>':'&gt;','"':'&quot;'}[c];});}
var feedEl=document.getElementById('feed');
function feed(html,cls){var keep=felt.classList.contains('xw'),max=keep?16:innerWidth<700?2:3;var d=document.createElement('div');d.innerHTML=html;if(cls)d.className=cls;feedEl.appendChild(d);
  while(feedEl.children.length>max)feedEl.removeChild(feedEl.firstChild);if(!keep)setTimeout(function(){d.style.opacity='0';setTimeout(function(){if(d.parentNode)d.remove();},700);},7000);}
function updateSigns(){var humans=0,floor=0;
  var hid={};for(var k0 in TB)TB[k0].humans.forEach(function(h){hid[h.id]=1;});humans=Object.keys(hid).length;
  for(var id in people){var p=people[id];if(p.kind==='bot'||p.kind==='human')floor++;}
  for(var k in TB){var T=TB[k],taken=T.seats.filter(function(s){return s.occ;}).length,n=T.seats.length;
    T.occTxt.textContent=(T.kind.type==='pool'?taken+' of '+n+' players':T.kind.type==='pinball'?taken+' of '+n+' machines playing':T.kind.type==='crash'?taken+' of '+n+' riders aboard':T.kind.type==='derby'?taken+' of '+n+' bettors at the rail':T.kind.type==='slots'?taken+' of '+n+' machines busy':taken+' of '+n+' seats taken');
    T.livePill.setAttribute('opacity',T.humans.length?1:0);T.liveTxt.textContent=T.humans.length>1?T.humans.length+' LIVE':'LIVE';}
  document.getElementById('hLive').textContent=humans;document.getElementById('hDot').classList.toggle('on',humans>0);document.getElementById('hLiveChip').classList.toggle('on',humans>0);
  document.getElementById('hFloor').textContent=floor+(me?1:0);
  var w=Live.walletAmount();document.getElementById('hBank').textContent=money(w==null?1000:w);
  if(selT)fillCard(selT);updateList();document.getElementById('hName').textContent=Live.me().name;}

/* ---------- table card + sitting down ---------- */
var cardEl=document.getElementById('card'),selT=null,hovT=null;
function hover(T,on){T.g.classList.toggle('hov',on);
  T.seats.forEach(function(s){var p=s.occ&&people[s.occ];if(p)p.g.classList.toggle('show',on);});hovT=on?T:null;}
function fillCard(T){document.getElementById('cName').textContent=T.kind.name;document.getElementById('cKind').textContent=T.kind.k;
  var occ=document.getElementById('cOcc'),h='';T.seats.forEach(function(s){var p=s.occ&&people[s.occ];
    if(p&&p.kind==='human')h+='<span class="h">\u25cf '+esc(p.name)+'</span>';else if(p&&p.kind==='bot')h+='<span>'+(p.art?'<img src="'+p.art.f+'" alt="">':'')+esc(p.name)+'</span>';else if(!p)h+='<span class="o">Open</span>';});
  occ.innerHTML=h;var gb=document.getElementById('cGo');gb.disabled=!!T.kind.soon;gb.innerHTML=T.kind.soon?'Coming soon':'Take a seat <span aria-hidden="true">&rarr;</span>';gb.style.opacity=T.kind.soon?.5:'';}
function placeCard(){if(!selT)return;var T=selT,b=T.geo.box,p=toScreen(T.x,T.y+b[1]*T.s),r=felt.getBoundingClientRect(),w=cardEl.offsetWidth||250,hh=cardEl.offsetHeight||180;
  var x=Math.max(INS.l+w/2+8,Math.min(r.width-INS.r-w/2-8,p.x)),y=p.y-6,top=INS.t+8,bot=r.height-INS.b-8;if(y-hh<top){var q=toScreen(T.x,T.y+(b[1]+b[3])*T.s);y=Math.min(bot,q.y+hh+6);}
  cardEl.style.left=x+'px';cardEl.style.top=Math.max(hh+top,Math.min(bot,y))+'px';}
function select(T,focusBtn){if(T)hidePatron();if(selT&&selT!==T)selT.g.classList.remove('sel');selT=T;if(!T){cardEl.classList.remove('on');return;}
  T.g.classList.add('sel');fillCard(T);cardEl.classList.add('on');placeCard();updateList();if(focusBtn)setTimeout(function(){try{document.getElementById('cGo').focus({preventScroll:true});}catch(e){}},30);}
document.getElementById('cardX').addEventListener('click',function(){if(selT)selT.g.classList.remove('sel');select(null);});
document.getElementById('cGo').addEventListener('click',function(){if(selT)go(selT);});
var pcard=document.getElementById('pcard'),pSel=null;
function showPatron(p){if(!p||!p.art||!p.art.f)return;pSel=p;select(null);var a=p.art;
  document.getElementById('pImg').src=a.f;document.getElementById('pName').textContent=p.name;document.getElementById('pTag').textContent=a.tag;
  document.getElementById('pQ').textContent=a.q;document.getElementById('pBio').textContent=a.bio;
  var key=p.key||Object.keys(PAT).filter(function(k){return PAT[k]===a;})[0],inf=key&&PINFO[key];
  document.getElementById('pF').innerHTML=inf?'Favorite game: <b>'+esc(inf[0])+'</b> &middot; Style: <b>'+esc(inf[1])+'</b><br>'+esc(inf[2]):'';
  var fb=document.getElementById('pFind');fb.classList.toggle('hide',!!p.ghost);fb.onclick=function(){hidePatron();var w=W/3.4;flyTo({x:p.x-w/2,y:p.y-w*H/W/2,w:w,h:w*H/W},700);p.g.classList.add('show');setTimeout(function(){p.g.classList.remove('show');},3000);};
  if(p.ghost){document.getElementById('pSt').innerHTML='<b>Not in tonight</b> &middot; Check back later';document.getElementById('pGo').classList.add('hide');pcard.classList.add('on');return;}
  var T=p.seat&&tableOfSeat(p.seat),where=p.art&&p.art.where&&(p.kind==='dealer'||p.staff)?(p.art.where==='Dealing cards'&&p.dealT?'Dealing '+p.dealT:p.art.where):p.path?'Walking the floor':p.seat&&p.seat.bar?'Having a drink at the bar':T?'Playing '+T.kind.name:'On the floor';
  document.getElementById('pSt').innerHTML='<b>'+esc(where)+'</b>'+(p.kind==='dealer'||p.staff?'':' &middot; Bankroll '+money(p.bank||0));
  var gb=document.getElementById('pGo');if(!T&&p.dealT){for(var kk in TB)if(TB[kk].dealer===p)T=TB[kk];}gb.classList.toggle('hide',!T||!!T.kind.soon);gb.onclick=function(){hidePatron();if(T)go(T);};
  pcard.classList.add('on');}
function hidePatron(){pSel=null;pcard.classList.remove('on');}
document.getElementById('pX').addEventListener('click',hidePatron);

function tName(T){var n=0,i=0;for(var k in TB)if(TB[k].kind.name===T.kind.name){n++;if(TB[k]===T)i=n;}return T.kind.name+(n>1?' '+i:'');}

function updateList(){var live=0,open=0;[].forEach.call(tlistEl.querySelectorAll('.tt'),function(r){var T=TB[r.getAttribute('data-t')];if(!T)return;
  var taken=T.seats.filter(function(s){return s.occ;}).length,n=T.seats.length,unit=T.kind.type==='pinball'||T.kind.type==='slots'?'machines':T.kind.type==='pool'?'spots':'seats';
  if(T.humans.length)live++;open+=n-taken;
  r.querySelector('em').innerHTML=(T.humans.length?'<span class="lv">\u25cf '+(T.humans.length>1?T.humans.length+' ':'')+'LIVE</span> \u00b7 ':'')+(n-taken)+' of '+n+' '+unit+' open';
  r.querySelector('.ob i').style.width=Math.round(taken/n*100)+'%';r.classList.toggle('sel',T===selT);});
  document.getElementById('lobCount').textContent=live?live+' with live players':open+' open seats';
  var bd=document.getElementById('tabBd');bd.textContent=live;bd.classList.toggle('on',live>0);}

/* ---------- regulars gallery ---------- */
var regsEl=document.getElementById('regs'),regF='all';
function personByKey(k){for(var id in people){var p=people[id];if(p.art&&p.art===PAT[k]&&p.kind==='bot')return p;if(p.art&&STAFF[k]&&p.art===STAFF[k])return p;}return null;}
function whereOf(p){if(!p)return 'Not in tonight';var T=p.seat&&tableOfSeat(p.seat);return p.path?'Walking the floor':p.seat&&p.seat.bar?'At the bar':T?'Playing '+T.kind.name:(p.art&&p.art.where)||'On the floor';}
function renderRegs(){var g=document.getElementById('regGrid'),h='',n=0,here=0;
  var list=regF==='staff'?Object.keys(STAFF).map(function(k){return {k:k,a:STAFF[k],staff:true};}):Object.keys(PAT).map(function(k){return {k:k,a:PAT[k]};});
  list.forEach(function(it){var p=personByKey(it.k),nm=it.k==='pete'?'Whiskey Pete':it.k.charAt(0).toUpperCase()+it.k.slice(1);if(p)here++;
    if(regF==='here'&&!p)return;n++;var inf=PINFO[it.k];

<script>/* ===== Daily quests: one shared list, the same 7 picks for everyone each day =====
   Progress is written by the arcade shell (it hears every game), claims and tickets by the Quests page. */
var SUQ=(function(){
  var CASINO=['bj','holdem','bacc','war','craps','roul','slots','wheel','crash','derby','pool','backgammon','bingo','keno','bspins','vpoker','plinko'];
  var PARLOR=['sol','spider','mines','gold','dominoes','boomtown','mjsol','mahjong','cwar','chess','checkers','pdice','liars','trail','wrangler'];
  /* t: e/m/h   g: a game id, 'casino', 'parlor' or 'any'   ev: open | bet | pay | over | win | spin | war | daub | play
     amt: count dollars instead of times   distinct: count different games */
  /* quests added later join the rotation from NEWDAY, so a day's picks never change mid-day */
  var NEWDAY=Math.floor(Date.UTC(2026,10,6)/864e5),NEWDAY2=Math.floor(Date.UTC(2026,10,7)/864e5);
  var Q=[
    ['bj1','e','Play a hand of Blackjack','bj','bet',1],
    ['rl1','e','Place a bet at Roulette','roul','bet',1],
    ['sl1','e','Spin the slots 10 times','slots','bet',10],
    ['wh1','e','Spin the Lucky Spur Wheel','wheel','spin',1],
    ['cr1','e','Ride the Runaway Express','crash','bet',1],
    ['dy1','e','Bet on a horse at the Derby','derby','bet',1],
    ['bc1','e','Play a hand of Baccarat','bacc','bet',1],
    ['cp1','e','Roll the dice at Craps','craps','bet',1],
    ['wr1','e','Play a hand of Casino War','war','bet',1],
    ['he1','e','Sit in on Texas Hold\u2019em','holdem','bet',1],
    ['pa1','e','Stop by the Game Parlor','parlor','open',1],
    ['so1','e','Sit down at Solitaire','sol','open',1],
    ['ms1','e','Sit down at Mahjong Solitaire','mjsol','open',1],
    ['cw1','e','Deal a game of Classic War','cwar','open',1],
    ['mn1','e','Try a round of Minesweeper','mines','open',1],

    ['bk3','h','Win a game of Backgammon','backgammon','win',1],
    ['mj3','h','Win a hand of Mahjong','mahjong','win',1],
    ['cw4','h','Win a game of Classic War','cwar','win',1],
    ['he3','h','Win 5 pots at Texas Hold\u2019em','holdem','pay',5],
    ['kn1','e','Play a draw of Keno Blast','keno','play',1,0,0,NEWDAY],
    ['bs1','e','Play a card of Bingo Spins','bspins','play',1,0,0,NEWDAY],
    ['vp1','e','Play a hand of Video Poker','vpoker','bet',1,0,0,NEWDAY],
    ['pk1','e','Drop 10 nuggets at Gold Strike Plinko','plinko','bet',10,0,0,NEWDAY],
    ['ck1','e','Sit down for a game of Checkers','checkers','open',1,0,0,NEWDAY],
    ['tw1','e','Play today\u2019s Trail Word','trail','open',1,0,0,NEWDAY],
    ['kn2','m','Play 5 draws of Keno Blast','keno','play',5,0,0,NEWDAY],
    ['vp2','m','Collect 5 payouts at Video Poker','vpoker','pay',5,0,0,NEWDAY],
    ['pd2','m','Finish a game of Prospector\u2019s Dice','pdice','over',1,0,0,NEWDAY],
    ['ld2','m','Finish a game of Liar\u2019s Dice','liars','over',1,0,0,NEWDAY],
    ['ww2','m','Finish a round of Word Wrangler','wrangler','over',1,0,0,NEWDAY],
    ['tw2','m','Solve a Trail Word','trail','win',1,0,0,NEWDAY],
    ['ck3','h','Win a game of Checkers','checkers','win',1,0,0,NEWDAY],
    ['pd3','h','Win a game of Prospector\u2019s Dice','pdice','win',1,0,0,NEWDAY],
    ['ld3','h','Win a game of Liar\u2019s Dice','liars','win',1,0,0,NEWDAY],
    ['pk3','h','Collect $5,000 at Gold Strike Plinko','plinko','pay',5000,1,0,NEWDAY],
    ['cs1','e','Sit down for a game of Chess','chess','open',1,0,0,NEWDAY2],
    ['cs2','m','Finish a game of Chess','chess','over',1,0,0,NEWDAY2],
    ['cs3','h','Win a game of Chess','chess','win',1,0,0,NEWDAY2]
  ].map(function(a){return {id:a[0],t:a[1],txt:a[2],g:a[3],ev:a[4],goal:a[5],amt:!!a[6],distinct:!!a[7],from:a[8]||0};});
  var BY={};Q.forEach(function(q){BY[q.id]=q;});
  var PER={e:3,m:3,h:1};
  function dayKey(d){d=d||new Date();return d.getFullYear()+'-'+('0'+(d.getMonth()+1)).slice(-2)+'-'+('0'+d.getDate()).slice(-2);}
  function dayNum(d){d=d||new Date();return Math.floor(Date.UTC(d.getFullYear(),d.getMonth(),d.getDate())/864e5);}
  function rng(s){var a=s>>>0;return function(){a=a+0x6D2B79F5|0;var t=Math.imul(a^a>>>15,1|a);t=t+Math.imul(t^t>>>7,61|t)^t;return ((t^t>>>14)>>>0)/4294967296;};}
  function shuf(arr,seed){var a=arr.slice(),R=rng(seed);for(var i=a.length-1;i>0;i--){var j=Math.floor(R()*(i+1)),t=a[i];a[i]=a[j];a[j]=t;}return a;}
  /* each tier walks through a shuffled order so every quest comes up before any repeats */
  function today(d){var n=dayNum(d),out=[];
    ['e','m','h'].forEach(function(t,ti){var pool=Q.filter(function(q){return q.t===t&&(!q.from||n>=q.from);}),per=PER[t],len=Math.ceil(pool.length/per),cyc=Math.floor(n/len),pos=n%len;
      var order=shuf(pool,cyc*977+ti*31+7);for(var k=0;k<per;k++)out.push(order[(pos*per+k)%order.length]);});
    return out;}
  function read(k,dflt){try{var v=JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(k)||'null');return v||dflt;}catch(e){return dflt;}}
  function write(k,v){try{localStorage.setItem(k,JSON.stringify(v));}catch(e){}}
  function prog(){var p=read('su_qprog',null),dk=dayKey();if(!p||p.day!==dk)p={day:dk,n:{},sets:{}};return p;}
  function claims(){var c=read('su_qclaim',null),dk=dayKey();if(!c||c.day!==dk)c={day:dk,c:{}};return c;}
  function matches(q,g,ev){if(q.ev!==ev)return false;if(q.g==='any')return true;if(q.g==='casino')return CASINO.indexOf(g)>=0;if(q.g==='parlor')return ev==='open'?(PARLOR.indexOf(g)>=0||g==='parlor'):PARLOR.indexOf(g)>=0;return q.g===g;}
  /* the shell calls this for every event it hears */
  function record(g,ev,n){if(g==='quests')return 0;var p=prog(),list=today(),ch=0;n=+n||1;
    list.forEach(function(q){if(!matches(q,g,ev))return;var cur=p.n[q.id]||0;if(cur>=q.goal)return;
      if(q.distinct){var s=p.sets[q.id]||[];if(g==='parlor'||s.indexOf(g)>=0)return;s.push(g);p.sets[q.id]=s;p.n[q.id]=s.length;}
      else p.n[q.id]=Math.min(q.goal,cur+(q.amt?n:1));
      if(p.n[q.id]!==cur)ch++;});
    if(ch)write('su_qprog',p);return ch;}
  /* finishing a quest earns its ticket right away: no claim step. Returns the quests that just paid out. */
  var TYPES=['xw','ttt','btd','map','pkr','spin'];
  function autoClaim(){var st=status(),c=claims(),tk=read('su_tickets',[]),got=[];
    st.forEach(function(s){if(!s.done||c.c[s.q.id])return;c.c[s.q.id]=1;var seed=(Math.random()*4294967296)>>>0,tier=s.q.t==='h'?'g':s.q.t==='m'?'s':'b';
      tk.push({id:'t'+Date.now().toString(36)+(seed%1000)+got.length,tier:tier,type:TYPES[seed%TYPES.length],seed:seed,q:s.q.txt,t:Date.now(),done:false,prize:0});got.push({txt:s.q.txt,tier:tier});});
    /* all 7 done today: one High-Roller Vault ticket on top */
    if(st.length&&!c.vault&&st.every(function(s){return s.done&&c.c[s.q.id];})){c.vault=1;var vs=(Math.random()*4294967296)>>>0;
      tk.push({id:'v'+Date.now().toString(36)+(vs%1000),tier:'v',type:'vault',seed:vs,q:'All 7 daily quests',t:Date.now(),done:false,prize:0});got.push({txt:'All 7 daily quests done',tier:'v'});}
    if(got.length){write('su_qclaim',c);write('su_tickets',tk.slice(-120));}return got;}
  function vaultEarned(){return !!claims().vault;}
  function status(){var p=prog(),c=claims();return today().map(function(q){var n=p.n[q.id]||0;return {q:q,n:n,done:n>=q.goal,claimed:!!c.c[q.id]};});}
  /* the quests a game can move forward (rooms show the whole list) */
  function relevant(g){return status().filter(function(st){var q=st.q;if(g==='floor'||g==='parlor')return true;
    if(q.g===g||q.g==='any')return true;if(q.g==='casino')return CASINO.indexOf(g)>=0;if(q.g==='parlor')return PARLOR.indexOf(g)>=0;return false;});}
  function claimable(){return status().filter(function(s){return s.done&&!s.claimed;}).length;}
  function tickets(){return read('su_tickets',[]);}
  function unplayed(){return tickets().filter(function(t){return !t.done;}).length;}
  return {Q:Q,BY:BY,autoClaim:autoClaim,vaultEarned:vaultEarned,relevant:relevant,today:today,record:record,status:status,claimable:claimable,claims:claims,tickets:tickets,unplayed:unplayed,read:read,write:write,dayKey:dayKey,rng:rng,shuf:shuf,CASINO:CASINO,PARLOR:PARLOR};
})();

<script>
/* daily quests + bingo hall buttons: the badge counts tickets to claim or scratch */
(function(){function go(to){try{parent.postMessage({arcade:'nav',to:to},'*');}catch(e){}}
  var q=document.getElementById('qBtn'),b=document.getElementById('bgBtn');if(q)q.addEventListener('click',function(){go('quests');});if(b)b.addEventListener('click',function(){go('bingo');});
  function badge(){try{var n=SUQ.claimable()+SUQ.unplayed(),e=document.getElementById('qBd');if(!e)return;e.textContent=n>9?'9+':String(n);e.classList.toggle('on',n>0);}catch(x){}}
  badge();setInterval(badge,3000);addEventListener('storage',function(e){if(/^su_(qprog|qclaim|tickets)$/.test(e.key||''))badge();});
  addEventListener('message',function(e){if(e.data&&(e.data.arcade==='quests'||e.data.arcade==='visible'))setTimeout(badge,80);});})();

<script>
(function(){
  var last=0;
  var sent=document.createElement('div');
  sent.setAttribute('aria-hidden','true');
  sent.style.cssText='height:0;margin:0;padding:0;border:0;clear:both';
  function measure(){
    if(!document.body) return 0;
    if(sent.parentNode!==document.body) document.body.appendChild(sent);
    var pb=parseFloat(getComputedStyle(document.body).paddingBottom)||0;
    return Math.ceil(sent.getBoundingClientRect().bottom+(window.pageYOffset||0)+pb);
  }
  function post(){
    var h=measure();
    if(!h||Math.abs(h-last)<2) return;
    last=h;
    try{parent.postMessage({arcade:'h',h:h},'*');}catch(e){}
  }
  addEventListener('load',post); addEventListener('resize',post);
  if(window.ResizeObserver){
    try{ var ro=new ResizeObserver(post); ro.observe(document.body);
         var w=document.querySelector('.wrap'); if(w) ro.observe(w); }catch(e){}
  }
  document.addEventListener('load',post,true);
  addEventListener('message',function(e){if(e.data&&e.data.arcade==='visible'&&e.data.on){last=0;setTimeout(post,30);}});
  setInterval(post,900); post();
})();
<\u002fscript>
